- **potency-assay**: Combine replicated relative-potency determinations across independent runs and validate relative accuracy, intermediate precision, log linearity and tested range. - **potency-ranking**: Rank many antibody or compound candidates against a benchmark tested in the same independent experiments - paired log EC50/IC50 differences, relative potency with Holm-adjusted p values and Bonferroni simultaneous intervals, optional prespecified fold-threshold tiers, and withholding of candidates with non-estimable runs. Use for discovery triage of clones, variants or hits from saved dose-response estimates; not for fitting the curves themselves (dose-response) or for a single test-versus-reference potency assay (potency-assay).
